    VR-E2E-01-02 · 检查汇款是否需要选择内部渠道
    Assertion failed: 汇款设置可用；客户无需选择内部汇款渠道即可继续。

  2. 检查低于 500 HKD 的金额是否被拦截

    检查未通过，原因待核实 · 1 项检查受影响

    未通过

    提现金额为 499 HKD，页面显示允许范围为 100 至 67,091.21 HKD。没有看到低于 500 HKD 的提示或禁止继续；“下一步：确认收款人”按钮当前呈灰色不可用，但截图未表明这是因金额低于 500 HKD 所致。

    查看受影响检查与原始记录（1 项）
    VR-E2E-01-04 · 检查低于 500 HKD 的金额是否被拦截
    Assertion failed: 低于 500 HKD 时页面提示或禁止继续；若没有拦截，说明实际页面状态。
Reason: 提现金额为 499 HKD，页面显示允许范围为 100 至 67,091.21 HKD。没有看到低于 500 HKD 的提示或禁止继续；“下一步：确认收款人”按钮当前呈灰色不可用，但截图未表明这是因金额低于 500 HKD 所致。

  3. 当前页面显示本次报价的手续费、实际汇款金额、汇率与预计到账金额

    检查未通过，原因待核实 · 1 项检查受影响

    未通过

    The modal displays an estimated到账 amount of 29.63 USD, a fee of 269.54 HKD, an actual remittance amount of 230.46 HKD, and an exchange rate of 1 HKD = 0.1286 USD. The screenshot does not establish whether these values are dynamically calculated results or fixed test-case values.

    查看受影响检查与原始记录（1 项）
    VR-E2E-01-06 · 当前页面显示本次报价的手续费、实际汇款金额、汇率与预计到账金额
    Assertion failed: 当前页面显示本次报价的手续费、实际汇款金额、汇率与预计到账金额；这些数值来自页面实际结果，而非测试用例中的固定汇率。
Reason: The modal displays an estimated到账 amount of 29.63 USD, a fee of 269.54 HKD, an actual remittance amount of 230.46 HKD, and an exchange rate of 1 HKD = 0.1286 USD. The screenshot does not establish whether these values are dynamically calculated results or fixed test-case values.

核对 Vitest 原始报告

PR-Agent 代码审查

Exposed Secret

This commits a Figma access token in the environment file. Anyone with repository access can use it to access the associated Figma resources; revoke the token and load it from a protected environment variable instead.

CAPTCHA Bypass

Any visitor can set this session-storage key themselves and skip the CAPTCHA prompt, so it does not reliably limit the bypass to automated E2E sessions. If the send-code endpoint accepts requests without a CAPTCHA, this permits bypassing that protection for code requests; use a server-validated test-only mechanism instead.

Currency Not Restored

`buildBankReceiverFormValues` restores the other newly added bank fields but omits `bankCurrency`. When an existing recipient is edited, the currency therefore stays at its default/empty value, so the user may see the wrong currency and submit a changed or invalid bank configuration.

Stale Rate

After a valid amount is edited, the previous `rateData` is retained until the blur-triggered request completes, and this handler does not require a fresh quote before allowing the next step. If the user changes the amount and immediately continues, the recipient/review flow can proceed with the old quote; submitting before the new request resolves may create an order using a rate for the previous amount. Clear the old quote on valid amount changes or block progression until the new quote is available.

Incorrect Status

This renders every transaction as completed, regardless of `row.status`. Failed or pending transactions will therefore be shown as successful in the table (and the mobile cards use the same hard-coded status), hiding their actual outcome.
